def AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible : {n : ℕ} →
  LT.lt.{0} 0 n →
    (P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ) →
      (∀ (i j : Fin n), LE.le.{0} 0 (P i j)) →
        (∀ (i : Fin n), Eq.{1} (∑ j, P i j) 1) →
          (∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen.{0} (fun x y => LT.lt.{0} 0 (P y x)) a b) →
            AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n :=
fun {n} hn P hP hrow hirr =>
  { P := P, nonneg := hP, row_sum := hrow,
    pmin := Finset.inf'.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.positiveEntries P) ⋯ fun ij => P ij.1 ij.2, pmin_pos := ⋯,
    le_pmin := ⋯, irreducible := hirr }

def AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.choose : {n K : ℕ} →
  {i : Fin n} → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState n K i → Fin K :=
fun {n K} {i} p s =>
  if h : Finset.Nonempty.{0} s.unseen then Finset.min'.{0} s.unseen h
  else AnvBandit.topArg (AnvBandit.Execution.LocalState.index p s) s.active ⋯

  Membership.mem.{0, 0} S a ∧

        Membership.mem.{0, 0} S k →
          Eq.{1} (AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex N u k) (AnvBandit.ExecutionUCB.extendedIndex N u a) →
            LE.le.{0} a k :=

def AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardDistribution : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
    AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → AnvBandit.Execution.State n K → PMF.{0} (Fin n → Bool) :=
fun {n K} M p lam s =>
  AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.finiteProduct.{0, 0} fun i =>
    PMF.bernoulli (Subtype.mk.{1} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.stateMean M p lam s i) ⋯) ⋯

fun {n} C => AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.finiteProduct.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactRow C)

def AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → Fin 2 → ℝ :=

  HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.oracle M p.T lam)
    (∫ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n),
      AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.pathReward M p lam ω ∂AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.executionMeasure M p C lam)
def AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → Fin 2 → (Fin K → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c =>
  ∫ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n),
    AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.pathCost p c ω ∂AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.executionMeasure M p C lam
def AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → AnvBandit.Props :=
fun {n K} M p C =>
  {
    Pβ :=
      min.{0} (↑p.T)
        (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
          (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β),
    Aβ :=
      AnvBandit.Abeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p))
        p.β ↑p.T,
    M1 := AnvBandit.M1 ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star),
    DP := AnvBandit.DP ⋯ ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star),
    Kβ :=
      AnvBandit.concreteKbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) p.β
        (AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p) }
def AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → Fin 2 → (Fin K → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p C lam c =>
  AnvBandit.Ecost (AnvBandit.Eglob n (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Pβ (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam))
    (AnvBandit.Enode (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Aβ
      (M.Clam c p.L (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).DP lam))
    (AnvBandit.Epot (M.Flam c p.L lam) (M.supOthers c) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).M1
      (M.Flam (AnvBandit.Absorbing.unitCost M) p.L lam) (AnvBandit.Main.actualCaps M p C).Kβ)

          (∀ (a b : Fin n), Relation.ReflTransGen.{0} (fun x y => LT.lt.{0} 0 (P x y)) a b) →
            AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n :=
fun {n K} p P hP hrow hirr => AnvBandit.ContactMatrix.ofStochasticIrreducible ⋯ P hP hrow ⋯

                (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t)
                ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t) ν]
            ⇑(AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ (t : ℕ),
          LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
            AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
              ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib.{0, 0, 0} (fun ω => (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2)
                    (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1)
                    ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0}
                  (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1) ν]
                ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.const.{0, 0} (Prod.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Prefix n t) (Fin n → Bool))
                    (PMF.toMeasure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C)))) →
        (∀ (t : ℕ),
            LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
              ¬AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
                ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n) ∂ν, Eq.{1} (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2 id.{1}) →
          ∀ (c : Fin K → ℝ),
            (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
              Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
                LE.le.{0} (∫ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n), AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.pathCost p c ω ∂ν)
                  (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers c)) ↑p.T)
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam c)
                      (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ
                        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers c)) ↑p.T)))) :=
⋯

def AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.cooperativeBound : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n → Fin 2 → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p C lam =>
  AnvBandit.Uco (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑n ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam)
    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T)
    (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam))
    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T))
def AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.independentBound : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p lam =>
  AnvBandit.Uind (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑n ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam)
    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T) (M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) p.L lam)
    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T))
theorem AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.corollary_4_2 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2),
  LT.lt.{0} (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam)) (M.Flam (M.rho p.T lam) p.L lam) →
    LT.lt.{0}
        (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.Main.actualEcost M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam))
          (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T)))
        (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) (M.supOthers (M.rho p.T lam))) ↑p.T)
          (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑n ↑p.T) (M.Gamma lam))) →
      LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam)
          (AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.cooperativeBound M p C lam) ∧
        LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.BaselineTrajectory.teamRegret M p lam)
            (AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.independentBound M p lam) ∧
          LT.lt.{0} (AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.cooperativeBound M p C lam)
            (AnvBandit.BaselineComparison.independentBound M p lam) :=
⋯

def AnvBandit.Absorbing.prefixGood.{u_1} : {n K : ℕ} →
  {Ω : Type u_1} →
    AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
      AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool) → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) → ℕ → Ω → Prop :=
fun {n K} {Ω} M p lam X J t ω =>
  Membership.mem.{u_1, u_1}
    (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1} (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.selected.{u_1} p X J)
      (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.reward.{u_1} X) (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.mean.{u_1} M p lam X J) t p.L)
    ω
def AnvBandit.Absorbing.live.{u_1} : {n K : ℕ} →
  {Ω : Type u_1} →
    AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
      AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool) → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) → ℕ → Ω → Prop :=
fun {n K} {Ω} M p lam X J j ω =>
  LT.lt.{0} (p.phaseEnd j) p.T ∧ AnvBandit.Absorbing.prefixGood.{u_1} M p lam X J (p.phaseEnd j) ω
def AnvBandit.Absorbing.state.{u_1} : {n K : ℕ} →
  {Ω : Type u_1} →
    AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
      AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K →
        Fin 2 → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool) → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) → ℕ → Ω → Finset.{0} (Fin n) :=
fun {n K} {Ω} M p lam X J j ω =>
  ite.{1} (AnvBandit.Absorbing.live.{u_1} M p lam X J j ω)
    (AnvBandit.Execution.holders M.star
      (AnvBandit.Execution.run p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) (p.phaseEnd j)))
    Finset.univ.{0}
def AnvBandit.Absorbing.error.{u_1} : {n K : ℕ} →
  {Ω : Type u_1} →
    AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
      AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool) → (Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) → ℕ → Ω → ℝ :=
fun {n K} {Ω} M p lam X J j ω =>
  ite.{1}
    (AnvBandit.Absorbing.live.{u_1} M p lam X J (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} j 1) ω ∧
      AnvBandit.Absorbing.wrong M.star
        (AnvBandit.Execution.preBoundary p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} j 1)))
    1 0

  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (t : ℕ)
  (f : Prod.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.Absorbing.Precontact n t) (Fin n → Fin n) → ℝ),

      (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.executionMeasure M p C lam) fun ω =>
      f
        (Prod.mk.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.Absorbing.beforeContact t ω)
          (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2)) =ᵐ[AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.executionMeasure M p C lam]
    fun ω =>
    ∫ (R : Fin n → Fin n),
      f
        (Prod.mk.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.Absorbing.beforeContact t ω)
          R) ∂PMF.toMeasure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C) :=
⋯

  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (lam : Fin 2) (t v : ℕ),

  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (j : ℕ),
  have t := HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (p.phaseEnd (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} j 1)) 1;

  ∑ i,

        ENNReal.ofReal
          (max.{0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.sigmaR hd ω i0 i) ↑j)
            0) ∂AnvBandit.procMeasure C (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} n 1)).toReal

      LE.le.{0} Rteam
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Gamma lam) (AnvBandit.Eglob n ρmax caps.Pβ Clam0))
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) ρmax) ↑T))) →
        LE.le.{0} Rteam
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Gamma lam) (AnvBandit.Enode ρmax caps.Aβ ClamDP))

                                            δ))

theorem AnvBandit.measure_adaptive_sum_ge_le.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} [inst : MeasurableSpace.{u_1} Ω]
  (μ : MeasureTheory.Measure.{u_1} Ω) [inst_1 : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure.{u_1} μ]
  [inst_2 : StandardBorelSpace.{u_1} Ω] (ℱ : MeasureTheory.Filtration.{u_1, 0} ℕ inst) (D X : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (muc : ℕ → ℝ),
  (∀ (t : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.{u_1, 0} (D t)) →
    (∀ (t : ℕ), MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.{u_1, 0} (X t)) →
      ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF.{u_1}

def AnvBandit.ReachData.ofIrreducible : {n : ℕ} →
  {P : Fin n → Fin n → ℝ} → AnvBandit.IsIrreducible P → (i0 : Fin n) → AnvBandit.ReachData P i0 :=
fun {n} {P} hP i0 =>
  have hex := ⋯;
  let rk := fun v => Nat.find ⋯;
  have rk_spec := ⋯;
  have rk_min := ⋯;
  have hrank_i0 := ⋯;
  have hrank_zero := ⋯;
  have hparent := ⋯;
  { rank := rk, rank_i0 := hrank_i0, rank_eq_zero := hrank_zero, rank_le := ⋯,
    parent := fun v => if hv : Eq.{1} v i0 then i0 else Classical.choose.{1} ⋯, edge_pos := ⋯, parent_lt := ⋯ }

      (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n)
        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (Real.exp 1) (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) 1) (AnvBandit.epsP C)))) :=
⋯

    LE.le.{0} 0 rH →
      LE.le.{0} 1 β →

                    (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) 1) (AnvBandit.epsP C))
                      β)))))) :=
⋯

theorem AnvBandit.terminal_budget : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K},
  LT.lt.{0} 0 n →
    ∀ (c : Fin K → ℝ) (L : ℝ),
      (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
        Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
          ∀ (J : Finset.{0} (Fin n)) (D : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)) (i0 : Fin n),
            Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →
              (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} J i → Disjoint.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} J i → LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (D i)) 2) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n),
                      Membership.mem.{0, 0} J i →
                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →
                    (∀ (i : Fin n),
                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} J i →
                          LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)))
                            (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                      LE.le.{0}
                        (∑ i ∈ J,
                          ∑ k ∈ Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star, M.wk c L k)
                        (M.Ccap c L) :=
⋯

            LE.le.{0} (∑ s ∈ Finset.range (c T), w s) (∑ t ∈ Finset.range T, HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑(x t)) (w (c t))) :=

  (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ),

            LE.le.{0} (↑(R.AplusCount T i k))

theorem AnvBandit.subset_selection_bound : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K} (R : AnvBandit.GoodRun M)
  (q : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℕ),
  (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ),
      LE.le.{0} 1 s → Eq.{1} (R.p i k s) (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.b k) (M.f k (q i k s)))) →
    (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 1 s → LE.le.{0} s (q i k s)) →
      (∀ (t : ℕ) (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K),
          Eq.{1} (R.I t i) k →
            Eq.{1} (q i k (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (R.N t i k) 1)) (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (R.teamCount t k) 1)) →
        LT.lt.{0} 0 R.L →
          ∀ (T : ℕ) {k : Fin K},
            Ne.{1} k M.star →
              ∀ (J : Finset.{0} (Fin n)),
                LE.le.{0} (∑ i ∈ J, ↑(R.AplusCount T i k))
                  (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑(Finset.card.{0} J) ↑(M.nkAll R.L k))
                    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} 4 (M.gap k)) (M.Xi k))) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.proposition_A_6 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (R : AnvBandit.GoodRun M)
  (q : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℕ),
  (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ),
      LE.le.{0} 1 s → Eq.{1} (R.p i k s) (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.b k) (M.f k (q i k s)))) →
    (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 1 s → LE.le.{0} s (q i k s)) →
      (∀ (t : ℕ) (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K),
          Eq.{1} (R.I t i) k →
            Eq.{1} (q i k (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (R.N t i k) 1)) (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (R.teamCount t k) 1)) →
        LT.lt.{0} 0 R.L →
          ∀ (T : ℕ) {k : Fin K},
            Ne.{1} k M.star →
              LE.le.{0} (∑ i, R.EikPers q T i k) (M.Xi k) ∧
                (∀ (i : Fin n),
                    LE.le.{0} (↑(R.AplusCount T i k))
                      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (↑(M.nkAll R.L k))
                        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} 4 (M.gap k)) (R.EikPers q T i k)))) ∧
                  ∀ (J : Finset.{0} (Fin n)),
                    LE.le.{0} (∑ i ∈ J, ↑(R.AplusCount T i k))
                      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑(Finset.card.{0} J) ↑(M.nkAll R.L k))
                        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} 4 (M.gap k)) (M.Xi k))) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.lemma_C_2_traj : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K} (R : AnvBandit.GoodRun M) (c : Fin K → ℝ)
  (T : ℕ) (arr : Fin n → ℕ) (D : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)) (elim : Fin n → ℕ) (i0 : Fin n),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →
        (∀ (i : Fin n), Disjoint.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
          (∀ (i : Fin n), LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (D i)) 2) →
            (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n),
                  LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i))) (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                (∀ (t : ℕ) (i : Fin n),
                    LE.le.{0} (arr i) t → LT.lt.{0} t T → Membership.mem.{0, 0} (R.active t i) M.star) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n),
                      LE.le.{0}
                        (Finset.card.{0}
                          {k |
                            k ∉ Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i) ∧
                              ∃ t, Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Finset.range T) t ∧ LE.le.{0} (arr i) t ∧ Eq.{1} (R.I t i) k})
                        (elim i)) →
                    LE.le.{0} (∑ t ∈ Finset.range T, ∑ i, c (R.I t i))
                      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                        (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) ↑(∑ i, min.{0} T (arr i)))
                          (M.Ccap c R.L))
                        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.wmax c R.L) ↑(∑ i, elim i))) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.lemma_C_2.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} [inst : MeasurableSpace.{u_1} Ω]
  (μ : MeasureTheory.Measure.{u_1} Ω) {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (L : ℝ) (T : ℕ)
  (loss Arr El : Ω → ℝ) (Aβ DP : ℝ),
  MeasureTheory.Integrable.{0, u_1} loss μ →
    MeasureTheory.Integrable.{0, u_1}
        (fun ω =>
          HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) (Arr ω)) (M.Ccap c L))
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.wmax c L) (El ω)))
        μ →
      Eq.{1}
          (∫ (ω : Ω),
            HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) (Arr ω)) (M.Ccap c L))
              (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.wmax c L) (El ω)) ∂μ)
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) Aβ) (M.Ccap c L))
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.wmax c L) DP)) →
        (∀ᵐ (ω : Ω) ∂μ,
            LE.le.{0} (loss ω)
              (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) (Arr ω)) (M.Ccap c L))
                (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.wmax c L) (El ω)))) →
          LE.le.{0} (∫ (ω : Ω), loss ω ∂μ)
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c) Aβ) (M.Clam c L DP 0)) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.abel_bound : ∀ {d u : ℕ → ℝ},
  Eq.{1} (d 0) 0 →

              HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.posPart (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (d (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1)) (d ℓ)))
                (u (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1))) :=

theorem AnvBandit.lemma_C_3 : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (L : ℝ) (lam : Fin 2) (Ph : ℕ)
  (d u : ℕ → ℝ) (M1 Kbeta Eloss : ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    LE.le.{0} 0 M1 →
      Eq.{1} (d 0) 0 →
        (∀ (ℓ : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 0 (d ℓ)) →
          (∀ (ℓ : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 0 (u ℓ)) →
            LE.le.{0} Eloss
                (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Flam c L lam)
                  (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c)
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                      (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} 2 M1)
                        (M.Flam (fun k => ite.{1} (Eq.{1} k M.star) 0 1) L lam))
                      (∑ ℓ ∈ Finset.range Ph,
                        HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (d (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1))
                          (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (u (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1)) (u (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 2))))))) →
              LE.le.{0}
                  (∑ ℓ ∈ Finset.range Ph,
                    HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.posPart (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (d (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1)) (d ℓ)))
                      (u (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} ℓ 1)))
                  Kbeta →
                LE.le.{0} Eloss
                  (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Flam c L lam)
                    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (M.supOthers c)
                      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                        (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} 2 M1)
                          (M.Flam (fun k => ite.{1} (Eq.{1} k M.star) 0 1) L lam))
                        Kbeta))) :=
⋯

def AnvBandit.DecayModel.ofPositiveCurves : {n K : ℕ} →
  (b : Fin K → ℝ) →
    (f : Fin K → ℕ → ℝ) →
      (star : Fin K) →
        (∀ (k : Fin K), LT.lt.{0} 0 (b k)) →
          (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} (b k) 1) →
            (∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 1 s → LE.le.{0} 0 (f k s)) →
              (∀ (k : Fin K) (s t : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 1 s → LE.le.{0} s t → LE.le.{0} (f k t) (f k s)) →
                (∀ (k : Fin K), Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} (f k) Filter.atTop.{0} (nhds.{0} 0)) →
                  (∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ), LE.le.{0} 1 s → LE.le.{0} (f k s) (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} 1 (b k))) →
                    (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} (b k) (b star)) →
                      (∀ (k : Fin K), Eq.{1} (b k) (b star) → Eq.{1} k star) →
                        LE.le.{0} 2 n → LE.le.{0} 2 K → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K :=
fun {n K} b f star b_pos b_le_one f_nonneg f_anti f_tendsto f_le_gap b_le_star star_unique two_le_n two_le_K =>
  { b := b, f := fun k => AnvBandit.extendPositiveCurve (f k), star := star, b_pos := b_pos, b_le_one := b_le_one,
    f_nonneg := ⋯, f_anti := ⋯, f_tendsto := ⋯, f_le_gap := ⋯, b_le_star := b_le_star, star_unique := star_unique,
    two_le_n := two_le_n, two_le_K := two_le_K }

theorem AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.measure_good_compl_le_finite.{u_1} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} [inst : MeasurableSpace.{u_1} Ω]
  {n K T : ℕ} (μ : MeasureTheory.Measure.{u_1} Ω) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure.{u_1} μ]
  (F : MeasureTheory.Filtration.{u_1, 0} ℕ inst) (selected : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → Ω → Bool)
  (X p : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → Ω → ℝ),
  LE.le.{0} 2 n →
    LE.le.{0} 2 K →
      LE.le.{0} 1 T →
        (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ),
            LT.lt.{0} t T → MeasurableSet.{u_1} (setOf.{u_1} fun ω => Eq.{1} (selected i k t ω) true)) →
          (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ), LT.lt.{0} t T → MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.{u_1, 0} (X i k t)) →
            (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ), LT.lt.{0} t T → MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable.{u_1, 0} (p i k t)) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ),
                  LT.lt.{0} t T → ∀ (ω : Ω), Eq.{1} (X i k t ω) 0 ∨ Eq.{1} (X i k t ω) 1) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ),
                    LT.lt.{0} t T → ∀ (ω : Ω), Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Set.Icc.{0} 0 1) (p i k t ω)) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (t : ℕ),
                      LT.lt.{0} t T → MeasureTheory.condExp.{u_1, 0} (↑F t) μ (X i k t) =ᵐ[μ] p i k t) →
                    ∀ (δ : ℝ),
                      LT.lt.{0} 0 δ →
                        LE.le.{0} δ 1 →
                          LE.le.{0}
                            (MeasureTheory.Measure.real.{u_1} μ
                              (compl.{u_1}
                                (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1} selected X p T
                                  (Real.log
                                    (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0}
                                      (↑(HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} 2 n) K) T))
                                      δ)))))
                            δ :=
⋯
structure AnvBandit.GoodRunOn {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (T : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 4
fields:
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.L : ℝ

  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.bhat : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℝ
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.meanSum : Fin n → Fin K → ℕ → ℝ
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.mean_bounds : ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
      LE.le.{0} 1 m →
        LE.le.{0} m (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I T i k) →
          LE.le.{0} (M.b k) (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (self.meanSum i k m) ↑m) ∧
            LE.le.{0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (self.meanSum i k m) ↑m)
              (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.b k) (AnvBandit.psi (M.f k) m))
  AnvBandit.GoodRunOn.hc : ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K) (m : ℕ),
      LE.le.{0} 1 m →
        LE.le.{0} m (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I T i k) →
          LE.le.{0} (abs.{0} (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (self.bhat i k m) (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (self.meanSum i k m) ↑m)))
            (AnvBandit.rdelta self.L m)

      LT.lt.{0} t T →
        ∀ (i : Fin n),
          Membership.mem.{0, 0} (self.active t i) M.star →

              LE.le.{0} 1 (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i)) ∧
                LE.le.{0} 1 (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star) ∧
                  LE.le.{0}
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (self.bhat i M.star (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star))
                      (AnvBandit.rdelta self.L (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i M.star)))
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (self.bhat i (self.I t i) (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i)))
                      (AnvBandit.rdelta self.L (AnvBandit.pullCount self.I t i (self.I t i))))

def AnvBandit.IsExponential : {n K : ℕ} → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → (Fin K → ℝ) → (Fin K → ℝ) → Prop :=
fun {n K} M a eta =>
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (a k)) ∧
    (∀ (k : Fin K), LT.lt.{0} 0 (eta k)) ∧
      ∀ (k : Fin K) (s : ℕ),
        LE.le.{0} 1 s →
          Eq.{1} (M.f k s)
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (a k)
              (Real.exp (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (Neg.neg.{0} (eta k)) (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (↑s) 1))))

  (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (X : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool)
  (J : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) (ω : Ω) (t : ℕ),
  LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
    ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : Fin K),
      Membership.mem.{u_1, u_1}
          (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1} (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.selected.{u_1} p X J)

          ω →

            ((AnvBandit.Execution.run p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t).nodes i).active M.star →
          Eq.{1} (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i) k →
            LE.le.{0} 1 (AnvBandit.Execution.N p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i k) →
              LE.le.{0}
                (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑(AnvBandit.Execution.N p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i k))
                  (M.gap k))
                (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                  (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0}
                      (↑(AnvBandit.Execution.N p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i k))
                      (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} (M.gap k) 2))
                    (AnvBandit.ActualPublicBudget.area M
                      (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω)) t i k))
                  (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0}

theorem AnvBandit.ActualPublicBudget.terminal_statistical_budget : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K),

            Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →

                  (∀ (i : Fin n),
                      Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →

                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →

                            (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                      LE.le.{0}
                        (∑ i ∈ U,
                          ∑ k ∈ Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star, M.wkN c L k)
                        (M.CNcap c L) :=

theorem AnvBandit.ActualPublicBudget.terminal_public_selected_cost.{u_1} : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {Ω : Type u_1}
  (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (X : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool)
  (J : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) (ω : Ω) (c : Fin K → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      ∀ (U : Finset.{0} (Fin n)) (D : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)) (i0 : Fin n),
        Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →
          (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → Disjoint.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
            (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (D i)) 2) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n),
                  Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                    Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n),
                    Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                      LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)))
                        (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                  ∀ (S : Fin n → Fin K → Finset.{0} ℕ),
                    (∀ (i : Fin n),
                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                          ∀ (k : Fin K),
                            Membership.mem.{0, 0}
                                (Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) k →
                              ∀ (t : ℕ),
                                Membership.mem.{0, 0} (S i k) t →
                                  LT.lt.{0} t p.T ∧
                                    Membership.mem.{0, 0}
                                        ((AnvBandit.Execution.run p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω)
                                                t).nodes
                                            i).active
                                        M.star ∧
                                      Eq.{1} (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i)
                                          k ∧
                                        Membership.mem.{u_1, u_1}
                                          (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1}
                                            (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.selected.{u_1} p X J)
                                            (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.reward.{u_1} X)
                                            (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.mean.{u_1} M p 1 X J) t p.L)
                                          ω) →
                      LE.le.{0}
                        (∑ i ∈ U,
                          ∑ k ∈ Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star,
                            HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (c k) ↑(Finset.card.{0} (S i k)))
                        (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.CNcap c p.L) (M.Bpub c)) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.ActualPublicBudget.terminal_lost_public_selected_cost.{u_1} : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {Ω : Type u_1}
  (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (X : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool)
  (J : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) (ω : Ω) (c : Fin K → ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      ∀ (U : Finset.{0} (Fin n)) (D : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)) (i0 : Fin n),
        Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →
          (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → Disjoint.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
            (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (D i)) 2) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n),
                  Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                    Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n),
                    Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                      LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)))
                        (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                  ∀ (Lost : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)) (elim : Fin n → ℕ),
                    (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → Lost i ⊆ M.others) →
                      (∀ (i : Fin n),
                          Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                            Disjoint.{0} (Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star)
                              (Lost i)) →
                        (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i → LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Lost i)) (elim i)) →
                          ∀ (S : Fin n → Fin K → Finset.{0} ℕ),
                            (∀ (i : Fin n),
                                Membership.mem.{0, 0} U i →
                                  ∀ (k : Fin K),
                                    Membership.mem.{0, 0}
                                        (Union.union.{0}
                                          (Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star)
                                          (Lost i))
                                        k →
                                      ∀ (t : ℕ),
                                        Membership.mem.{0, 0} (S i k) t →
                                          LT.lt.{0} t p.T ∧
                                            Membership.mem.{0, 0}
                                                ((AnvBandit.Execution.run p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω))
                                                        (J ω) t).nodes
                                                    i).active
                                                M.star ∧
                                              Eq.{1}
                                                  (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω)
                                                    t i)
                                                  k ∧
                                                Membership.mem.{u_1, u_1}
                                                  (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1}
                                                    (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.selected.{u_1} p X J)
                                                    (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.reward.{u_1} X)
                                                    (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.mean.{u_1} M p 1 X J) t p.L)
                                                  ω) →
                              LE.le.{0}
                                (∑ i ∈ U,
                                  ∑
                                    k ∈
                                      Union.union.{0}
                                        (Finset.erase.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star)
                                        (Lost i),
                                    HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (c k) ↑(Finset.card.{0} (S i k)))
                                (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                                  (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.CNcap c p.L)
                                    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (∑ i ∈ U, ↑(elim i)) (M.wNmax c p.L)))
                                  (M.Bpub c)) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.ActualPublicBudget.stable_tail_public_cost.{u_1} : ∀ {n K : ℕ} {Ω : Type u_1}
  (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K) (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (X : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Bool)
  (J : Ω → ℕ → Fin n → Fin n) (ω : Ω) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (Tstart : ℕ) (i0 : Fin n) (D : Fin n → Finset.{0} (Fin K)),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      Membership.mem.{u_1, u_1}
          (AnvBandit.ObservedPrefix.good.{u_1} (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.selected.{u_1} p X J)
            (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.reward.{u_1} X) (AnvBandit.ExecutionBounds.mean.{u_1} M p 1 X J) p.T p.L)
          ω →
        (∀ (t : ℕ) (i : Fin n),
            LE.le.{0} Tstart t →
              LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
                Eq.{1} ((AnvBandit.Execution.run p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t).nodes i).active
                  (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i))) →
          Membership.mem.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i0) M.star →
            (∀ (i : Fin n), Disjoint.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) →
              (∀ (i : Fin n), LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (D i)) 2) →
                (∀ (i : Fin n), Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)) M.star) →
                  (∀ (i : Fin n),
                      LE.le.{0} (Finset.card.{0} (Union.union.{0} (AnvBandit.permSet n K i) (D i)))
                        (AnvBandit.smax n K)) →
                    LE.le.{0}
                      (∑ t ∈ Finset.range p.T with LE.le.{0} Tstart t,
                        ∑ i, c (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (X ω)) (J ω) t i))
                      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.CNcap c p.L) (M.Bpub c)) :=

def AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.choose : {n K : ℕ} →
  {i : Fin n} → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.Params n K → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState n K i → Fin K :=
fun {n K} {i} p s =>
  if h : Finset.Nonempty.{0} s.unseen then Finset.min'.{0} s.unseen h
  else AnvBandit.topArg (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.LocalState.index p s) s.active ⋯
def AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.step : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.Params n K →
    ℕ → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.State n K → (Fin n → ℝ) → (Fin n → Fin n) → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.State n K :=
fun {n K} p t s x _j => ite.{1} (LT.lt.{0} t p.T) (AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.observe p s x) s
def AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.stateMean : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
    AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.Params n K → Fin 2 → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.State n K → Fin n → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p lam s i =>
  have k := AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.actions p s i;
  M.mean k (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} 1 (ite.{1} (Eq.{1} lam 0) ((s.nodes i).N k) (∑ j, (s.nodes j).N k)))
def AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.outcomeDistribution : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K →
    AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.Params n K →
      Fin 2 → AnvBandit.BaselineExecution.State n K → PMF.{0} (AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.Outcome n) :=
fun {n K} M p lam s =>
  PMF.map.{0, 0} (fun x => Prod.mk.{0, 0} x id.{1}) (AnvBandit.BaselineLaw.rewardDistribution M p lam s)

theorem AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss_global_personal_candidate : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (cmax : ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} (c k) cmax) →
        LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam c)
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
              (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam))
              (M.Ccap c p.L))
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) ↑p.T))) :=
⋯
def AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.pathCost : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → (Fin K → ℝ) → AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n → ℝ :=
fun {n K} p c ω =>
  ∑ t ∈ Finset.range p.T,
    ∑ i,
      c
        (AnvBandit.Execution.I p (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.realTape (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.pathRewards ω))
          (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.pathContacts p ω) t i)

  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (cmax : ℝ),
  (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} 0 (c k)) →
    Eq.{1} (c M.star) 0 →
      (∀ (k : Fin K), LE.le.{0} (c k) cmax) →
        LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.goodLoss M p C lam c)
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) (AnvBandit.ActualWaiting.expectedWaiting M p C lam))
            (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam)) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss_le_goodLoss_add_failure : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2) (c : Fin K → ℝ) (cmax : ℝ),

theorem AnvBandit.GlobalTheorem.team_regret_global_bound : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2),
  have c := M.rho p.T lam;
  have cmax := M.supOthers c;
  have Pbar :=
    min.{0} (↑p.T)
      (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H) (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p))
        p.β);
  LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam)
    (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑n ↑p.T)
      (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Gamma lam)
        (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) ↑p.T)
          (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) Pbar) (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam))
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) ↑p.T)))))) :=
⋯
theorem AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret_conversion : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2),
  LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.teamRegret M p C lam)
    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Gamma lam) (AnvBandit.GlobalRegret.weightedLoss M p C lam (M.rho p.T lam))) :=
⋯
def AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.oracle : {n K : ℕ} → AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → ℕ → Fin 2 → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M T lam => ite.{1} (Eq.{1} lam 0) (AnvBandit.Vstar0 M) (AnvBandit.Vstar1 M)
def AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.pathReward : {n K : ℕ} →
  AnvBandit.DecayModel n K → AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K → Fin 2 → AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n → ℝ :=
fun {n K} M p lam ω =>
  AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.jointValue M lam (AnvBandit.TrajectoryExpectation.pathActions p ω)
theorem AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_team_regret_global_bound : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2)
  (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n)) [inst : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure.{0} ν],
  Eq.{1} (MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} 0) ν)
      (MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} 0)
        (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.observedMeasure M p C lam)) →
    (∀ (t : ℕ),
        LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
          ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib.{0, 0, 0} (fun ω => (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1)
                (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t)
                ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t) ν]
            ⇑(AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ (t : ℕ),
          LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
            AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
              ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib.{0, 0, 0} (fun ω => (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2)
                    (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1)
                    ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0}
                  (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1) ν]
                ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.const.{0, 0} (Prod.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Prefix n t) (Fin n → Bool))
                    (PMF.toMeasure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C)))) →
        (∀ (t : ℕ),
            LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
              ¬AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
                ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n) ∂ν, Eq.{1} (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2 id.{1}) →
          have c := M.rho p.T lam;
          have cmax := M.supOthers c;
          have Pbar :=
            min.{0} (↑p.T)
              (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C (AnvBandit.Execution.holder p M.star) (↑p.H)
                (↑(AnvBandit.ActualPropagation.preparation M p)) p.β);
          LE.le.{0} (AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.originalTeamRegret M p lam ν)
            (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} ↑n ↑p.T)
              (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (M.Gamma lam)
                (min.{0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) ↑p.T)
                  (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0}
                    (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) Pbar)
                      (M.Clam c p.L 0 lam))
                    (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} p.δ (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (↑n) cmax) ↑p.T)))))) :=
⋯

theorem AnvBandit.ObservedRegret.original_integral_eq : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (p : AnvBandit.Execution.Params n K) (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (lam : Fin 2)
  (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n)) [inst : MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure.{0} ν],
  Eq.{1} (MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} 0) ν)
      (MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} 0)
        (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.observedMeasure M p C lam)) →
    (∀ (t : ℕ),
        LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
          ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib.{0, 0, 0} (fun ω => (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1)
                (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t)
                ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t) ν]
            ⇑(AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.rewardKernel M p lam t)) →
      (∀ (t : ℕ),
          LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
            AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
              ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib.{0, 0, 0} (fun ω => (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2)
                    (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1)
                    ν) =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.Measure.map.{0, 0}
                  (fun ω => Prod.mk.{0, 0} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} t ω) (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).1) ν]
                ⇑(ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.const.{0, 0} (Prod.{0, 0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Prefix n t) (Fin n → Bool))
                    (PMF.toMeasure.{0} (AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.contactDistribution C)))) →

            LT.lt.{0} t p.T →
              ¬AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.retainedContact p (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1) →
                ∀ᵐ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n) ∂ν, Eq.{1} (ω (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} t 1)).2 id.{1}) →
          ∀ (F : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n → ℝ),

                Eq.{1} (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} p.T ω) (Preorder.frestrictLe.{0, 0} p.T ω') → Eq.{1} (F ω) (F ω')) →

                Eq.{1} (∫ (ω : AnvBandit.ExecutionLaw.Path n), F ω ∂ν)

theorem AnvBandit.DecayModel.Pbeta_general_horizon : ∀ {n K : ℕ} (M : AnvBandit.DecayModel n K)
  (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) (i0 : Fin n) {T H : ℕ} {β : ℝ},
  LE.le.{0} 1 T →
    LE.le.{0} 1 H →
      LT.lt.{0} 1 β →
        LE.le.{0}
          (AnvBandit.Pbeta ⋯ C i0 (↑H) (↑(AnvBandit.lastShortPhase (↑H) β ↑(M.Bschedule (AnvBandit.LdeltaStar n K T))))
            β)
          (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0}
            (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} (AnvBandit.propagationMomentCoefficient n β (AnvBandit.epsP C))
              (max.{0} 1
                (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} (HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} M.Bcoef (AnvBandit.logConfidenceCoefficient n K))
                  (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} β (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} β 1)))))
            (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (↑H)
              (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0}
                (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} (Real.log (HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} (↑T) 1))
                  (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} β (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} β 1)))
                (HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} (↑H)
                  (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} (HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} β (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} β 1)) 1))))) :=
⋯

theorem AnvBandit.lintegral_sigmaAllE_eq_capped : ∀ {n : ℕ} {hd : LT.lt.{0} 0 (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} n 1)},
  LE.le.{0} 2 n →
    ∀ (C : AnvBandit.ContactMatrix n) {i0 : Fin n} (R0 : AnvBandit.ReachData C.P i0) {A : Finset.{0} (Fin n)},
      Membership.mem.{0, 0} A i0 →
        ∀ (i : Fin n),
          Eq.{1} (∫⁻ (R : ℕ → Fin n → Fin n), AnvBandit.sigmaAllE R A i ∂AnvBandit.stepMeasure C)
            (∫⁻ (ω : ℕ → Fin (HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} n 1) → Fin n → Fin n),
